# Battle art: every battle piece still to make

*Persona 3: Solstice · split out Nov 7 2026 (Kat's plan): all the battle pieces in one place, to make together at the end, after `FEMC-SKIN-ART.md`. Until a piece exists the fight uses the fighter's usual version, so nothing breaks while these wait. The old design sheet is in `_archive/FEMC-SKINS-BATTLE-DESIGN-old.md`.*

**What's here (updated Nov 14, after art batch 159: Stella, Sailor Moon, Draculaura and Sukajan are done):** 372 prompts for 60 looks: 58 second pose sheets, 59 Theurgy cut-ins, 75 "1 MORE!" pictures (Solar's looks get two), 60 entrance cards, 59 finish cards, 59 signature effect sheets; plus the foxfire theme sheet and frame (on hold), the curio sticker sheet (Nov 14) and two cell-4 clean-ups. Evoker close-ups are parked in `FEMC-SKINS-EVOKER-PROMPTS.md`; optional redos in `FEMC-SKINS-REMAKES.md`.

**How:** pick a look; its pieces are in order. Attach the files on each **Attach** line in that order (all in `Femc Mode Images/`), paste the prompt, save into **New Adds** under its **Save as** name and tell Claude. Collab looks: make their pieces after the outfit is in the game. Each look has a **look bible** (palette, vibe, motifs, light, scene); the prompts follow it.

**Finish-card words** (unless a prompt says otherwise): Solar **RISE AND SHINE**, Haechan **TOO EASY**, Jungwon **MEETING ADJOURNED**, Akihiko **ON TO THE NEXT BOUT.**, Junpei **THAT'S THE GAME!**, Shinjiro **DON'T WASTE YOUR BREATH.**, Aigis **TARGET NEUTRALIZED**, Minji **THAT'S A WRAP**, Yukari **THERE YOU HAVE IT.**, Mitsuru **KNEEL BEFORE ME.**, Jihoon **YOU'RE WELCOME**, Koromaru **WOOF! WOOF! WOOF!**

### Backgrounds

| Kind of picture | Background |
|---|---|
| Pose sheets and HUD frames | **Transparent** |
| Theme effect sheets and signature effect sheets (N) | **Solid black**: glowing effects come out better keyed from black than with ChatGPT's transparency (soft glows turn muddy or get cut off). |
| Theurgy cut-ins, finish cards, entrance cards, eye strips, couple Showtimes, Evoker close-ups | **Their own full background** (they fill the screen) |

If a transparent one comes back with a background anyway, that's fine: Claude cuts it out.

### If something comes back wrong

| Problem | Say |
|---|---|
| Poses overlap (pose sheet) | "Redo it with more empty space between the poses." |
| Face or Persona moved (Theurgy cut-in) | "Keep the first image's layout exactly; change only the clothes and hair." |
| Eyes too small (eye strip) | "Zoom in much closer: only the eyes and eyebrows should fill the banner." |
| Outfit changed (entrance card, Showtime) | "Keep the outfit exactly as in the first image." |
| Words misspelled (finish card) | "Same picture; the words must read exactly …" (with the card's words) |
| Grid lines, boxes or transparency (theme or signature effect sheet) | "Redo it on solid black with no grid lines or boxes." |

Finish cards use the fighter's own finish line (listed at the top of each fighter). To give a look its own words, change the last sentence of its prompt.

---

## Solar

### Collab outfits

Make these after the outfit is in the game (its pose sheet and scene portraits from `FEMC-SKIN-ART.md`).

### Cardcaptor Sakura · `sk-collab-cardcaptor`

Couple set with Jungwon's Syaoran. A pink-and-white battle dress with a winged hat and a star wand; a playful short bob.

> **Look bible.** Palette: candy pink, white, gold and sky blue. Vibe: a magical girl out catching cards: brave, sweet, wings everywhere.  
> Motifs: pink stars, white feathers, sparkles and cherry blossom petals. Light: a soft pink magic glow at night. Scene: a moonlit town seen from the rooftops at night, a glowing magic circle underfoot.

#### 1. Theurgy cut-in

Attach: `Place Prompt Attachments/Theurgy - Solar.png`, then `Cardcaptor Sakura Cosplay [sk-collab-cardcaptor] - pose sheet.png` (your finished pose sheet, from `FEMC-SKIN-ART.md`), then `Place Prompt Attachments/Solar.png`  
Save as: `skin-sk-collab-cardcaptor-theurgy.png`
```
Cosplay of Sakura Kinomoto from Cardcaptor Sakura. Recreate the FIRST attached image, a full-screen 16:9 ultimate-attack cut-in, keeping its composition, camera angle, pose, Persona, speed lines and energy exactly, in the same Persona 3 Reload official art style: clean anime lineart, flat cel shading with hard shadows, a bold diagonal layout. Change only the character's outfit and hair to the look in the SECOND attached image: a pink-and-white puffy dress with a short bell skirt and white frills, a big pink bow at the chest, a white hat with small white wings and a pink ribbon, white gloves, white-and-pink boots with small wings at the ankles. Hair: her own jet-black hair cut into a short chin-length bob with two little face-framing strands tied with pink beads, sweet rose-pink tips. Same face as the THIRD attached image. Keep the Persona behind her exactly as in the first image, and keep her face in the same place in the frame. Repaint everything around the figure to this look instead of the first image's colours: the background becomes a moonlit town seen from the rooftops at night, a glowing magic circle underfoot in candy pink, white, gold and sky blue, the light is a soft pink magic glow at night, and pink stars and white feathers streak through the speed lines; the Persona keeps its own shape and design, lit to match. No text, no letters, no logos.
```

#### 2. "1 MORE!" eye strip

Attach: `skin-sk-collab-cardcaptor-portraits.png` (your finished scene portraits, from `FEMC-SKIN-ART.md`), then `Place Prompt Attachments/Solar.png`  
Save as: `skin-sk-collab-cardcaptor-onemore.png`
```
Cosplay of Sakura Kinomoto from Cardcaptor Sakura. A very wide, very short 6:1 banner image for a video-game "One More!" cut-in, in Persona 3 Reload official art style: clean anime lineart, flat cel shading with hard shadows. An extreme close-up of the SAME character as the attached images, cropped to just the eyes and eyebrows across the whole width (forehead and nose tip cut off by the top and bottom edges), looking sharply to the right with an intense, confident, determined expression. Her hair: her own jet-black hair cut into a short chin-length bob with two little face-framing strands tied with pink beads, sweet rose-pink tips, exactly as in the FIRST attached image, strands falling across the frame. Keep these from the look, exactly as in the FIRST attached image, wherever they show in the crop: a white hat with small white wings. Behind the face: bold flat diagonal stripes and speed lines in candy pink, white, gold and sky blue, lit by a soft pink magic glow at night, a few tiny pink stars and white feathers scattered in the stripes. No text, no letters, no logos, no border.
```

#### 6. Signature effect sheet (piece N)

Attach: `Place Prompt Attachments/Effect sheet - format.png` (format and spacing only)  
Save as: `skin-sk-collab-cardcaptor-fx.png`
```
Cosplay of Sakura Kinomoto from Cardcaptor Sakura. A sprite sheet of one video-game spell effect: a 3 × 2 grid of six square frames (3 columns, 2 rows), read left to right, top to bottom, on a pure solid black background (#000000). Every frame shows the SAME effect at a later moment, centred at the same point and the same scale, with a wide empty black margin (at least 10%) on every side so nothing touches or crosses into the next frame. Frame 1: one pink star flickers on in the centre. Frame 2: a few pink stars and white feathers appear around it, candy pink light gathering. Frame 3, the peak: a glowing magic circle opening in a ring of pink stars and white feathers from a gold-white centre. Frame 4: the pink stars and white feathers spreading wider, tumbling and turning. Frame 5: the pink stars and white feathers breaking into small gold sparkles. Frame 6: a few last tiny pink stars and white feathers and gold sparkles fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, candy pink, white, gold and sky blue, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

### Wednesday · `sk-collab-wednesday`

The black dance dress and the white collar, completely unimpressed; two long braids with one silver streak in the fringe.

> **Look bible.** Palette: black, white, storm grey and a touch of dark purple. Vibe: deadpan gothic: completely unimpressed.  
> Motifs: cobwebs, black spiders, raven feathers and rain. Light: cold grey storm light with a flash of lightning. Scene: a gothic school hall at night, rain on tall windows.

#### 1. Second pose sheet

Attach: `Wednesday Cosplay [sk-collab-wednesday] - pose sheet.png` (your finished pose sheet, from `FEMC-SKIN-ART.md`), then `Place Prompt Attachments/Solar.png`  
Save as: `skin-sk-collab-wednesday-posesheet-2.png`
```
Cosplay of Wednesday Addams from Wednesday. Character pose sheet: 3 columns × 2 rows on a transparent background (PNG with alpha), with clear empty space between every pose. The SAME person as the attached images in all six cells, full body, same face, same outfit, same hair, same weapon, same size, nothing overlapping. This is the SECOND pose sheet for the FIRST attached image: match its figure size, framing, camera angle, facing direction and art style exactly, with the feet on the same baseline in every cell. The outfit and hair are exactly as in the first image: a knee-length black dance dress with a layered ruffled tulle skirt and long sheer black sleeves, a crisp white Peter Pan collar and white cuffs, black tights, chunky black platform boots, a tiny black spider brooch. Hair: her own jet-black hair in two tight long braids and a razor-straight blunt fringe, one thin silver streak through the fringe. Her naginata's haft in matte black with a tiny black spider charm. Keep the red S.E.E.S. armband on the left arm if the first image has one (this is battle art). No skill effects anywhere: no glows, no magic, no sparkles, no motion lines, no ground shadows. Cell 1: casting a healing or support skill: standing tall, one open hand raised palm-up at chest height, calm and focused, the naginata held loosely in the other hand. Cell 2: guarding: braced low, the naginata held across her body like a shield, forearm raised, eyes on the enemy. Cell 3: dodging: a quick sidestep leaning away, weight on one foot, her hair and clothes swinging with the motion. Cell 4: knocked down: fallen to one knee with one hand on the ground, head low, still gripping the naginata. Cell 5: badly hurt: standing but hunched, one hand pressed to her side, breathing hard, the naginata lowered but not dropped, a determined look. Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, the naginata ready, her hair and clothes streaming back. Persona 3 Reload official art style. No text, no logos.
```

Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, the naginata ready, her hair and clothes streaming back. Cell 4: knocked down: fallen to one knee with one hand on the ground, head low, still gripping a knife. Cell 5: badly hurt: standing but hunched, one hand pressed to his side, breathing hard, the knives lowered but not dropped, a determined look. Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, a knife in each hand, his hair and clothes streaming back. Cosplay of Clawd from Monster High. A sprite sheet of one video-game spell effect: a 3 × 2 grid of six square frames (3 columns, 2 rows), read left to right, top to bottom, on a pure solid black background (#000000). Every frame shows the SAME effect at a later moment, centred at the same point and the same scale, with a wide empty black margin (at least 10%) on every side so nothing touches or crosses into the next frame. Frame 1: one claw mark flickers on in the centre. Frame 2: a few claw marks and fur tufts appear around it, varsity purple light gathering. Frame 3, the peak: three giant claw slashes tearing outward from a silver-white centre. Frame 4: the claw marks and fur tufts spreading wider, tumbling and turning. Frame 5: the claw marks and fur tufts breaking into small silver sparks. Frame 6: a few last tiny claw marks and fur tufts and silver sparks fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, varsity purple, black, tan and moon silver, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

Frame 5: the steam curls and embers breaking into small ember sparks. Frame 6: a few last tiny steam curls and embers and ember sparks fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, charcoal, apron navy, coat maroon and stove orange, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

> **Look bible.** Palette: Gekkoukan navy, ribbon red, white and steel silver. Vibe: mission: blend in.

#### 1. Second pose sheet

Character pose sheet: 3 columns × 2 rows on a transparent background (PNG with alpha), with clear empty space between every pose. The outfit and hair are exactly as in the first image: the Gekkoukan High winter uniform worn over her mechanical frame: a dark navy blazer with the school crest, a white shirt and a big red ribbon tied very neatly, a navy pleated skirt, her mechanical joints still showing at the wrists and knees. Keep the red S.E.E.S. armband on the left arm if the first image has one (this is battle art). No skill effects anywhere: no glows, no magic, no sparkles, no motion lines, no ground shadows. Cell 2: guarding: braced low, both forearms crossed in front of her like a shield, eyes on the enemy. Cell 3: dodging: a quick sidestep leaning away, weight on one foot, her hair and clothes swinging with the motion. Cell 4: knocked down: fallen to one knee with one hand on the ground, head low, one arm gun still raised. Cell 5: badly hurt: standing but hunched, one hand pressed to her side, breathing hard, her arm guns lowered but still aimed, a determined look. Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, both arm guns ready, her hair and clothes streaming back. ### Corpse Bride · `sk-collab-corpsebride`

Couple set with Jihoon's Victor: make both pose sheets in the same chat so the colours match. The ghost bride's tattered wedding gown and long torn veil; knee-length hair melting into pale ghost blue.

> **Look bible.** Palette: ghost blue, bone white, faded lace grey and butterfly blue. Vibe: a ghost bride: tragic, sweet and a little funny about it.  
> Motifs: blue butterflies, torn lace, floating candle flames and wilted wedding flowers. Light: pale blue moonlight with a soft ghostly glow. Scene: a crooked Victorian graveyard under a full moon, a bare tree heavy with blue butterflies.

Cell 5: badly hurt: standing but hunched, one hand pressed to her side, breathing hard, the bow lowered but not dropped, a determined look. Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, the bow ready, her hair and clothes streaming back. Persona 3 Reload official art style. No text, no logos.
```

Cosplay of Enid Sinclair from Wednesday. A sprite sheet of one video-game spell effect: a 3 × 2 grid of six square frames (3 columns, 2 rows), read left to right, top to bottom, on a pure solid black background (#000000). Every frame shows the SAME effect at a later moment, centred at the same point and the same scale, with a wide empty black margin (at least 10%) on every side so nothing touches or crosses into the next frame. Frame 1: one pastel heart flickers on in the centre. Frame 2: a few rainbow claw marks and little hearts appear around it, bubblegum pink light gathering. Frame 3, the peak: three bright rainbow claw slashes ripping out from a white-hot centre in a shower of little hearts. Frame 4: the rainbow claw marks and little hearts spreading wider, tumbling and turning. Frame 5: the rainbow claw marks and little hearts breaking into small pastel sparkles. Frame 6: a few last tiny rainbow claw marks and little hearts and pastel sparkles fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, bubblegum pink, sky blue, sunshine yellow and cream, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

> Motifs: black feathers, silver crosses, red apples and blank notebook pages. Light: cold white stage light with a red glow. Scene: a rainy city rooftop at night, giant blank billboards glowing behind.

Frame 5: the black raven shapes and dark energy ribbons breaking into small violet sparks. Frame 6: a few last tiny black raven shapes and dark energy ribbons and violet sparks fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, midnight indigo, black, gold and ruby red, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

Frame 4: the white crystal wing panels and snowflake hexagons spreading wider, tumbling and turning. Frame 5: the white crystal wing panels and snowflake hexagons breaking into small sky-blue snow. Frame 6: a few last tiny white crystal wing panels and snowflake hexagons and sky-blue snow fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, snow white, sky blue, pale pink and silver, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

Frame 1: one small water drop flickers on in the centre. Frame 2: a few water drops and bubbles appear around it, ice blue light gathering. Frame 3, the peak: a swirling wave of water and freezing mist bursting from a white-blue centre. Frame 4: the water drops and bubbles spreading wider, tumbling and turning. Frame 5: the water drops and bubbles breaking into small snowflakes. Frame 6: a few last tiny water drops and bubbles and snowflakes fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, ice blue, deep sea blue, white and silver, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

Frame 5: the dragon flames and teal fairy dust breaking into small sparkles. Frame 6: a few last tiny dragon flames and teal fairy dust and sparkles fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, sky blue, teal, gold and flame orange, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.

Frame 5: the music notes and sound waves breaking into small digital sparkles. Frame 6: a few last tiny music notes and sound waves and digital sparkles fading. Style: Persona 3 Reload battle VFX: sharp, stylish, graphic shapes with clean hard edges, white-hot cores, teal, silver grey, black and hot pink, high contrast, readable at small size. Pure light and energy only: no characters, no monster, no ground, no frame borders, no grid lines, no text or numbers, no watermark. Landscape 3:2.
```

> **Look bible.** Palette: moss olive, antique gold, sage cream and peridot green. Vibe: a garden romantic at dusk: soft, vintage and a little wild.  
> Motifs: lace vines, ivy leaves, antique gold cameos and peridot gems. Light: low golden dusk light through ivy. Scene: an overgrown cottage garden at dusk, ivy over old stone walls.

Hair: her own jet-black hair in a loose, curly romantic updo with soft curly tendrils falling around her face and neck, a few curls glinting with moss-green tips. The outfit and hair are exactly as in the first image: a black halter top printed all over with a fine spiderweb mesh pattern, ruched down the front with a keyhole at the chest and a small silver cross at its centre; a short tiered ruffled black mini skirt with silver chains draped across it, hung with little silver crosses; a black studded belt with an ornate silver western buckle and a cross stud; a black beaded necklace with a silver filigree cross; a spiked black choker; black-and-grey striped fingerless arm warmers to the elbow; black hoop earrings with chain ear cuffs; a black leather peaked cap with spikes and a buckle strap, tilted; black chunky lace-up platform boots. Hair: her own jet-black hair long, straight and sleek to her waist, the crown teased up into short spiky choppy pieces, two thin silver-grey streaks running through it like strands of web. Her naginata's shaft wrapped in black, a silver spiderweb filigree spread across the base of the blade, a small silver cross charm hanging below. Cell 1: casting a healing or support skill: standing tall, one open hand raised palm-up at chest height, calm and focused, the naginata held loosely in the other hand. Cell 2: guarding: braced low, the naginata held across her body like a shield, forearm raised, eyes on the enemy. Cell 3: dodging: a quick sidestep leaning away, weight on one foot, her hair and clothes swinging with the motion. Cell 4: knocked down: fallen to one knee with one hand on the ground, head low, still gripping the naginata. Cell 5: badly hurt: standing but hunched, one hand pressed to her side, breathing hard, the naginata lowered but not dropped, a determined look. Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, the naginata ready, her hair and clothes streaming back. Persona 3 Reload official art style. No text, no logos.

### Grunge Night · `sk-minji-grunge`

Joins Winter Lights (November–January). Kat's own look: a black halter tank, a tartan ruffle half-skirt over shredded cargo pants, silver chains and tartan platform boots.

> **Look bible.** Palette: grunge black, tartan red, chrome silver and dirty beige. Vibe: a punk show in the cold: shredded, layered and loud.  
> Motifs: silver chains, silver crosses, red tartan and skeleton hands. Light: red stage light cutting through a cold blue night. Scene: a snowy alley behind a club, Christmas lights strung over the back door.

#### 1. Second pose sheet

Attach: `skin-sk-minji-grunge-posesheet.png` (your finished pose sheet, from `FEMC-SKIN-ART.md`), then `Place Prompt Attachments/Minji.png`

Character pose sheet: 3 columns × 2 rows on a transparent background (PNG with alpha), with clear empty space between every pose. The SAME person as the attached images in all six cells, full body, same face, same outfit, same hair, same weapon, same size, nothing overlapping. This is the SECOND pose sheet for the FIRST attached image: match its figure size, framing, camera angle, facing direction and art style exactly, with the feet on the same baseline in every cell. The outfit and hair are exactly as in the first image: a black halter tank with a lace-trimmed neckline and a distressed white gothic filigree print (no legible lettering); a layered ruffle half-skirt draped at the hip over her pants, one tier in red-and-black tartan and one in beige plaid; baggy black wide-leg cargo pants, heavily shredded at the thighs with loose white threads, embroidered with a silver skeleton hand, with red tartan cross patches and strap-and-O-ring details; several silver chains slung round her hips; stacked chunky silver cuff bracelets hung with crosses, padlocks and oval lockets; a silver chain necklace with a cross pendant and little charms; black platform heeled ankle boots with red tartan panels, black ribbon laces and silver chains; her camera on a silver chain strap. Hair: her own long black hair, loose and messy, a few thin dark red streaks underneath. Cell 2: guarding: braced low, the bow held upright across her body like a shield, forearm raised, eyes on the enemy. Cell 3: dodging: a quick sidestep leaning away, weight on one foot, her hair and clothes swinging with the motion. Cell 4: knocked down: fallen to one knee with one hand on the ground, head low, still gripping the bow. Cell 5: badly hurt: standing but hunched, one hand pressed to her side, breathing hard, the bow lowered but not dropped, a determined look. Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, the bow in one hand and an arrow ready in the other, her hair and clothes streaming back. > Motifs: black lace, rosary beads, ornate crosses and silver studs. Light: candlelight in the dark with violet shadows. Scene: a candlelit chapel at midnight, black lace curtains at the windows.

Cell 3: dodging: a quick sidestep leaning away, weight on one foot, her hair and clothes swinging with the motion. Cell 5: badly hurt: standing but hunched, one hand pressed to her side, breathing hard, the bow lowered but not dropped, a determined look. Cell 6: dashing in: a full-speed sprint toward the same side the first sheet's attack pose faces, body low and leaning forward, the bow in one hand and an arrow ready in the other, her hair and clothes streaming back. #### Curio sticker sheet

Attach: nothing  
Save as: `curio-stickers.png`
```
A sticker sheet of five small game item icons in one row, evenly spaced with wide empty margins so nothing touches, on a transparent background (PNG with alpha). Each is a single object, seen slightly from above, in the same style as Persona 3 Reload item icons: clean bold outlines, flat cel shading with one soft highlight, readable at small size. Left to right: 1. an old lacquered music box, dark red with gold trim, its lid open and a tiny ballerina inside turned halfway; 2. a silver-backed hand mirror with a crack running corner to corner across the glass; 3. a small gold oval locket, open, a pressed violet flower behind its glass; 4. a silver pocket watch, open, both hands pointing to twelve; 5. a folded sheet of paper with a child's crayon drawing of a tall black tower, a yellow moon and a tiny figure waving at the top. No text, no labels, no numbers, no shadows on the ground, no background colour. Landscape 5:1.
```
